DnBOX 0.0.25.1 · SMK-37 Elite and Pro

MAIN · dev-checked

Owner-approved 0.0.25.1 packages. The owner reports successful use and recovery to 25.1; independent exact-package hardware validation for both models is not recorded here. Firmware reports 0.0.25, GIT 79280e76. Bundled Bluetooth controller included. The 600b 0.0.26 build was withdrawn. 0.0.26 at 94197fc8 is a hardware-test build on the nightly; it is not offered here.

DnBOX 0.0.26 — corrected boot-guard hardware test (94197fc8)

NIGHTLY · dev-checked

Owner-test candidate, not hardware-proven or production. Replaces the withdrawn 600b build; includes the reviewed retained-RAM boot-guard correction, SMK-37 identity, sample-integrity fixes and host fixture repair. Physical startup/restart and recovery still require owner testing. Keep a backup and the known-good 0.0.25.1 package. Exact-head Elite/Pro native builds PASS: app544232/544224B, free flash8440/8448B, pool8340B each. SDKV1.2.14/PADCAL0; bundledBTa334208b61552B CRC32564a8f55; package checks PASS. Full-suite raw434PASS/6FAIL/2NOT_RUN retained; subsequent focused checks close the four fixture failures, Lua,808/909 reference gaps and hiddenTB3PO gap; browser-only test N/A, not passed. Owner strict emu360 on unchanged d408 production inputs: Elite11406us/Pro11410us, zero late,648halves,3repeats. Not a measurement of these newly rebuilt image hashes. Boot-guard native checks and90seeded cases/model PASS, not hardware proof.

Elite and Pro (DN Box 0.0.25.1 79280e76: pattern chain uses the longest playing part)

NIGHTLY · dev-checked

DEVICE NOT TESTED. Source lane/025-zero-dropout 79280e763341d03ba43266d3e25dad66f12de860 (tree ab2e16a61c81abbcb6c4a71bd83bf0b5d943c120): a chain slot lasts for the longest playing part in the current pattern (909, 808, both 303s and BREAK), counting length and rate, rounded up to a whole 909 bar. Muted, part-off and empty parts do not extend the slot. Chaining stays live-only and is not saved. Firmware reports 0.0.25, GIT 79280e76. JieLi SDK V1.2.14, X0X_DEV_PADCAL=0. Elite_900 and Pro_900; app 547,880 B; RAM .data+.bss 95,556 of 98,304 B (2,748 B free); pool 335,744 of 344,064 B (8,320 B free, 8,192 B minimum). Bundled Bluetooth controller a334208b (61,552 B, CRC32 564a8f55); package_nightly --check exit 0 for both. Official emu360 gate (fm1-live, 360 MHz, 0 late during the test, worst under 11,609 µs): Elite 11,548 µs PASS, Pro 11,564 µs PASS. These are 52 µs and 76 µs above the 1ec5d683 figures (11,496 / 11,488); that sits inside the same-day passing run-to-run range (about 11,484–11,578 µs). Host suite: 415 ok, 0 FAIL, 1 skip (Pro package tests need a build.sh tree), 0 not-run; chain-longest-playing PASS. The previous 0.0.25 package (nightly-1ec5d683-dev) and 0.0.24.5 stay listed. Owner-operated test build, not production.

DEV / HARDWARE TEST — Elite and Pro (DN Box 0.0.25 1ec5d683: zero dropouts, Chicago accent +6 dB, combined 0.0.25)

NIGHTLY · dev-checked

DEVICE NOT TESTED. Source lane/025-zero-dropout 1ec5d683cd5d70ef99415509ff3460c33152bcca (tree 150c68305df079d2738785b5e5d3de1205df239e): combined 0.0.25 with zero-dropout cuts (808, 909/Miami, sequencer drain, 808 BD/SD on core 1, 32-frame same-block FX/master pipeline), Chicago accent (velocity-like level, +6 dB, 200 ms decay, accent sweep) and Chicago cuts. Firmware reports 0.0.25, GIT 1ec5d683. The only source change vs cd3128e45 is a test-only engine-brk-mute extract; Elite and Pro ELFs are byte-identical to cd3128e45 after swapping the 8-character GIT string. JieLi SDK V1.2.14, X0X_DEV_PADCAL=0. Elite_900 and Pro_900; app 547,496 B; RAM .data+.bss 95,556 of 98,304 B (2,748 B free); pool 335,744 of 344,064 B (8,320 B free, 8,192 B minimum). Bundled Bluetooth controller a334208b (61,552 B, CRC32 564a8f55); package_nightly --check exit 0 for both. Official emu360 gate (fm1-live, 360 MHz, 0 late during the test, worst under 11,609 µs): Elite 11,496 µs PASS, Pro 11,488 µs PASS. Same-gate ABBA vs 0.0.24.5 (8be16486) on cd3128e45: 0.0.24.5 Elite 20,736 µs / 420 late, Pro 21,270 µs / 424 late, FAIL; 0.0.25 is under the deadline with 0 late. Host suite on 1ec5d683: 413 ok, 1 FAIL (ctl-compile stdout/stderr order flake, same on two reruns, not firmware), 1 skip (Pro package tests need a build.sh tree), 0 not-run; engine-brk-mute PASS. Device tests remain NOT_RUN. Owner-operated test build, not production.
